# Page packets — Prosperity

47 pages, 6.625 x 10.25 inches. Made by the writers' room; every packet below is complete on its own.

## How to use these packets

1. **Start with the sheets** below: the character sheet, then the location sheet. Paste each,
   keep the results, and attach them as reference images with every page that names them
   (each page packet says which). Approve one page as your style page and attach that too.
   For a long book, train a LoRA on the approved sheets and pages; references alone drift.
2. **One page at a time.** Paste everything under a page's heading, nothing else. Keep the
   same model and settings for the whole book so the style and the characters stay put.
3. **No text on the pages.** The words are added afterwards, in the room, as a layer over
   your art - so a beautiful model that cannot spell is fine. If the model draws any letters,
   regenerate; do not keep a page with text on it.
4. **Check the page** against its checklist before you keep it. One thing wrong: regenerate
   with the same packet, and only then adjust wording.
5. **Upload each kept page** in the room (Production, Lettering tab) and run the lettering:
   the room draws every balloon and caption where the layout put them, and you download the
   finished, lettered page.

## Rules for every page

- Never render text of any kind: no balloons, captions, sound effects, page numbers,
  titles, chapter names, signatures, watermarks, nameplates or labels.
- Keep the areas each panel lists as clear: uncluttered art (sky, wall, shadow) where a
  balloon will sit, never a face or the thing the panel is about.
- Draw every character exactly as described, every time. The description wins over the
  previous page's picture.
- Panel count, panel shape and reading order are fixed by the map. Do not add, merge or
  reshape panels.

## Character sheet — draw this first

_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one character reference sheet: every character below standing full length in a row, front view, neutral pose, even daylight, plain light background, in the book's style. Same scale for everyone so heights compare. No text, names, labels or captions anywhere on the sheet.

**Style:**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

### Panel 1 — WIDE, EYE ANGLE

**Scene:** Night at a lakeside boathouse in 2099. Older Alex grips a mooring post as TJ steps from the dock and separates mid-stride; Alex's granddaughter stands behind him in a blanket. Leave the upper third of the dock and lake clear for sound lettering.

**Keep these areas clear — the lettering is added afterwards as a separate layer, so draw no words here, just uncluttered art with room for a balloon:**

**Scene:** Close on the granddaughter and older Alex, with a tanker barge and two siphon drones working beneath white lamps across Lake Keel. Keep their faces clear and leave the upper-left lake area open for dialogue.

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 1 (right) — Older Alex sees water being stolen from the restored lake.

**Panel 1.** NIGHT. A restored lake fills the old Keel mine. Terraces, gardens and workshops ring the shore. Older Alex stands on a boathouse dock with his granddaughter. Across the water, drones lower siphons from a tanker barge.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Grandpa. That’s not a fishing boat.

ALEX: No.

**Panel 2.** TJ, a small lopsided robot dog made from mismatched magnetic parts, springs from the dock. His head, body and legs separate in midair and continue forward.

SFX: KLAK-KLAK-KLAK

GRANDDAUGHTER: Is TJ broken?

ALEX: Not usually.

**Panel 3.** TJ’s separated pieces skim toward the barge. The siphon pulls a bright column of water upward. Dry channels scar the far shore.

**Panel 4.** A drone beam sweeps the dock. The granddaughter catches Alex’s sleeve. He watches TJ approach.

GRANDDAUGHTER: They’re stealing from us.

ALEX: Let’s see what they came to take.

PAGE CHECK: The restored future remains contested. Page turn reveals the thieves.

---
```

## Page 2 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. ## Page 2 (left) — The thieves reveal that the lake’s history is disputed.

```text
VOSS: Nobody gets water that clean by being polite.

NERA: Then he stole it first.

PAGE CHECK: The accusation is clear but unresolved.
```

```text
## Page 4 (left) — TJ sabotages the theft from inside the pump.

**Panel 1.** TJ’s head strains between the shutters. His legs reach through a maintenance gap.

SFX: KRRRNNK

**Panel 2.** Nera opens the service panel. A magnetic leg snaps onto the inner latch.

NERA: Something’s in the housing.

VOSS: Shut it down.

**Panel 3.** The siphon bucks. Water sprays across the deck. The tanker tilts.

SFX: WHUMP—KRAK

**Panel 4.** On the dock, the granddaughter grips the rail. Alex remains still.

GRANDDAUGHTER: He’s stuck.

ALEX: He knows the difference between stuck and working.

PAGE CHECK: TJ’s plan is legible through action.

---
```

## Page 5 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **THIEF 1** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **THIEF 2** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **GRANDDAUGHTER** — **Causality** - **Public role:** Small repair robot dog living with older Alex in the 2099 prologue. - **Immediate want:** Investigate and interfere with the drones stealing lake water. - **Deeper need / contradiction:** He is built from scraps but repeatedly acts as if usefulness is worth the risk of coming apart. - **Leverage / capability:** Can separate his head, legs and body segments to enter gaps, scout and manipulate machinery. - **Vulnerability:** Magnetic separation, missing parts, dark
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

## Page 7 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043.
```text
## Page 7 (right) — The granddaughter asks how Alex made the lake his.

**Panel 1.** Alex fits TJ’s body together. One leg is missing. The granddaughter holds the drone component.

GRANDDAUGHTER: How did you get this lake?

ALEX: I didn’t get it.

**Panel 2.** The component bears a commercial mark beside TJ’s scratches.

GRANDDAUGHTER: They say you made water pay for it.

ALEX: People say things when they want the water.

**Panel 3.** The lake reflects the terraces. The barge disappears.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Did you make them pay?

Alex studies the component.

ALEX: The work keeps accounts.

**Panel 4.** The component’s amber indicator blinks once. Alex notices but does not explain it.

PAGE CHECK: The future’s ownership question remains open, with a visual link to the story’s records and safeguards.

---
```

```text
## Page 8 (left) — Alex chooses not to chase the thieves.

**Panel 1.** The granddaughter points toward a service boat.

GRANDDAUGHTER: We can catch them.

ALEX: Not across open water.

**Panel 2.** Alex steadies TJ against the dock rail.

ALEX: We know what they took. We know what they left.

**Panel 3.** The lake settles. TJ’s head turns toward the retreating drones.

GRANDDAUGHTER: That’s enough?

ALEX: Enough to start.

**Panel 4.** Alex places the damaged component beside a public water-record terminal on the dock. The screen reads: `LAKE KEEL — DELIVERY RECORDS OPEN`.

PAGE CHECK: The frame is about evidence and accountability, not pursuit.
```

## Page 10 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **GRANDDAUGHTER** — **Causality** - **Public role:** Small repair robot dog living with older Alex in the 2099 prologue. - **Immediate want:** Investigate and interfere with the drones stealing lake water. - **Deeper need / contradiction:** He is built from scraps but repeatedly acts as if usefulness is worth the risk of coming apart. - **Leverage / capability:** Can separate his head, legs and body segments to enter gaps, scout and manipulate machinery. - **Vulnerability:** Magnetic separation, missing parts, dark
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 11 (right) — Seventeen-year-old Alex keeps Keel running before the debt transfer.

**Panel 1.** DAY. Keel pump hall: rust, narrow channels, stacked housing and dust. Alex presses his palm to a vibrating pump, head tilted.

**Panel 2.** He bolts a salvaged bracket over a cracked mount. The pump steadies.

SFX: THRUM
```

## Page 12 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **FOREMAN-7** — Foreman-7 is a broad-shouldered human Keel mine foreman in his forties, with dark brown skin, a shaved head, tired dark eyes and a square face mostly obscured by a dust mask and a white-and-amber Alpha-linked supervisory harness. He wears a reinforced charcoal mining coat, plated work trousers, steel-toed boots and a heavy helmet with a small control node at the throat; his silhouette is blocky, official and difficult to read. His signature detail is the white-and-amber node at
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is

```text
## Page 12 (left) — Alex’s seventeenth birthday becomes a legal trap.

`DEBT BALANCE: 418,607 CREDITS`  
`PROJECTED CLEARANCE: 28.7 YEARS`

**Panel 3.** Alex covers Adrian’s worn steel watch with his thumb.

FOREMAN-7: Happy birthday, Phantum.

ALEX: Twenty-eight point seven.

**Panel 4.** The shutter opens. Behind it, the Last Chance bar is closed and dust-covered.

PAGE CHECK: The immediate problem is public, specific and personal.
```

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 13 (right) — Ada gives Alex part of her ration and challenges his plan to leave.

**Panel 1.** At the pit rim, Ada offers Alex a battered mug.

ADA: I saved enough for one.

ALEX: There are two mugs.

ADA: I’m generous with containers.

**Panel 2.** They drink. Her thumb rests on the largest rivet of her shoulder strap.

ADA: You’re still leaving.

ALEX: Eventually.

**Panel 3.** Alex looks over Keel’s narrow water channels.

ADA: Keel’s not a sentence.

ALEX: The balance disagrees.

**Panel 4.** Ada studies him.

ADA: I’m your friend, Alex. Not your exit plan.

PAGE CHECK: Their friendship and non-romantic dynamic are explicit through action and dialogue.

---
```

## Page 15 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **GRANDMOTHER** — Grandmother Phantum is an elderly European-descended woman fully at home in Keel, small and frail but still capable, with soft pale-brown skin, wispy silver hair pinned loosely at the nape, cloudy blue-grey eyes and a lined face that can shift abruptly from confusion to piercing clarity. She wears a faded plum cardigan over a cream blouse, a long charcoal skirt, thick socks and soft house shoes; her silhouette is folded inward around a worn chair. Her signature detail is a smal

Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043.
```text
## Page 19 (right) — Adrian’s recording makes the inheritance morally complicated.

**Panel 1.** Adrian’s blue projection appears above the workbench.

ADRIAN: Alex. If you’re seeing this, I failed to come back.

**Panel 2.** Adrian looks directly toward Alex.

ADRIAN: I borrowed too much. I gambled some of it. I hurt you and Grandmother.

**Panel 3.** Alex grips the watch.

ADRIAN: The work was shared. The mistakes were mine.

**Panel 4.** The projection flickers.

ADRIAN: Question everything. Especially me.

PAGE CHECK: Adrian admits harm without turning the message into an explanation dump.

---
```

## Page 20 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ

```text
## Page 20 (left) — Alpha enters as a concrete restriction.

DISPLAY: `MOVEMENT AUTHORIZATION: TEMPORARILY SUSPENDED.`

BI11BOT: It knows your band stopped reporting.

BI11BOT: Alpha routes water, assigns work and records permission.

**Panel 4.** Ada looks at the locked door.

ADA: And if its records are wrong?

BI11BOT: Then the wrongness travels efficiently.

PAGE CHECK: Alpha is a distributed network, not a person.
```

```text
## Page 21 (right) — Alex chooses the first node despite the risk.

**Panel 1.** Bi11bot projects three locations along the infrastructure spine. The lowest glows inside Keel’s sealed mine.

BI11BOT: Three safeguards. The first is below Site 6.

**Panel 2.** Alex studies the glowing node.

ALEX: What does it do?

BI11BOT: It measures.

ALEX: That’s not much.

BI11BOT: It is when someone has been counting for you.

**Panel 3.** Ada folds the paper map into her strap.

ADA: We go together.

ALEX: You can stay out of it.

ADA: Not everything has to be yours because it started with your father.

**Panel 4.** Alex touches the mine location.

ALEX: Just checking.

PAGE CHECK: Alex commits. Ada chooses participation.

---
```

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. ## Page 23 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 23 (right) — Alex learns that the mine route will cost him.

**Panel 1.** In the workshop, Ada studies the map while Bi11bot folds into Alex’s tool bag.

BI11BOT: Who carries the downside?

ALEX: Usually the person with the broken part.

**Panel 2.** Alex budgets food, equipment and Grandmother’s care. Pilar takes the ledger.

PILAR: Medicine first. The rest can wait.

**Panel 3.** At Site 6, Tomas receives the old key’s travel token and parts advance.

TOMAS: Forty credits. Separate from your household account.

ALEX: I’ll repay it.

TOMAS: Make it real.

**Panel 4.** Alex and Ada approach the active mine gate.

PAGE CHECK: Tomas’s separate 40-credit note is clear.
```

## Page 25 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **B11** — (no description in the bible yet)

He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his late fifties, broad through the chest and waist from physical work, with weathered brown skin, close-cropped salt-and-black hair, a heavy grey moustache, deep-set dark eyes and a bruised leg after the Site 6 accident. He wears a dust-stiffened charcoal mining jacket, patched trousers, a reinforced work sleeve, heavy boots and a battered helmet; a carved balanced water-drop is cut into his wrist guard. His silhouette is solid, squ
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. - **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. ## Page 30 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **B11** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

## Page 33 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. Her silhouette is
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **TJ** — TJ is a small repair-robot dog assembled from visibly mismatched parts held together by magnets: a rounded head, four short magnetic legs, segmented body pieces, a flexible tail and a dog-shaped stance that remains recognizable even when separated. His surfaces are rusty grey, faded yellow and chipped blue, with scratched ceramic camera lenses; in 2099 his right eye carries a small heavy metal lens ring with a warm amber lens identical to Bi11bot's right-eye ring in 2043. His s

## Page 35 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force. - **ADA** — Ada Veyra is seventeen, mixed Indigenous Andean and European/criolla, with long wind-tangled copper hair, freckles, light teal-blue eyes, a strong Andean facial structure and a steady, rooted stance; she is lean, average height and physically sure-footed from growing up in Keel. She wears a dark high-collared top, patched charcoal trousers, sturdy boots, a rivet-studded strap across one shoulder, a muted moss-green wrap at her neck and a weathered travel pack. - **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his early forties in recordings and reconstructed memories, lean and slightly stooped from long hours at benches and terminals, with pale olive skin, dark brown hair going silver at the temples, a narrow jaw, tired hazel-brown eyes and the same watchful head tilt Alex inherited. He wears a rolled-sleeve charcoal work shirt, worn suspenders, dark trousers and a stained technical coat; his silhouette is angular, restless and surrounde
- **DIRECTOR** — Director Cassian Lock is a silver-templed Northern European-descended man in his early sixties, tall and lean with light skin, pale blue-grey eyes, a smooth square jaw and immaculate charcoal hair combed back from a high forehead. ## Page 38 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

She wears a faded green biofilter research coat over a rust-coloured shirt, rolled trousers, rubberized work shoes and clear protective sleeves; her silhouette is narrow and vertical among root beds, algae columns and abandoned desks. Her signature detail is a small living
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

## Page 40 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **REN** — Ren Okafor is a woman in her late thirties with a broad, muscular build, deep brown skin, a shaved head, dark eyes and a blunt, self-possessed face; her ancestry is not inferred from her surname. She wears a patched Orbital House fabrication suit with scorch marks, a heat-shielded apron, heavy work boots and a clipped respirator; her silhouette is broad-shouldered, grounded and visibly built for outer-rim machinery. Her signature detail is a pair of scorched red gloves tucked i
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder

```text
## Page 40 (left) — Lock offers Alex relief in exchange for control.

**Panel 1.** Director Cassian Lock approaches, examining the registry before Alex.

LOCK: An estate, a discrepancy and a debt. Efficiently connected.

**Panel 2.** Displays identify `CALDERA`, `VECTOR` and `ORBITAL` House functions.

**Panel 3.**

LOCK: Assign the estate to House administration. Your debt clears. Halyard access becomes permanent.

**Panel 4.** Alex looks at the debt display.

LOCK: Refuse, and immediate commercial rights freeze pending review.

ALEX: You’re asking me to sell work I didn’t make.

LOCK: I’m asking you to protect it at scale.

PAGE CHECK: Lock’s offer is genuinely attractive and receives an answer, not a disappearance.

---
```

```text
## Page 41 (right) — Alex discovers four living collaborators and their separate risks.

**Panel 1.** The registry opens four restricted contributor records: Lina, Suri, Jalen and Ren.

**Panel 2.** Dr. Lina Vey meets Alex and Ada beside a chemistry bench.

LINA: Adrian didn’t invent a purifier alone.

**Panel 3.** Suri holds a living root cutting. Jalen watches them in his collar mirror.

SURI: Mine dies if the flow is wrong.

JALEN: And mine gets flagged if the pattern is wrong.

**Panel 4.** Ren opens a scorched fabrication case.

REN: House review means shelving it or owning it.

ALEX: Then we build before they choose for us.

PAGE CHECK: Each collaborator has a personal reason to act.

---
```

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 42 (left) — The group chooses a safe, small objective.

**Panel 1.** In a rented workroom, the four components surround a cloudy Keel water sample.

ALEX: Keel has water now. It can’t use it.

**Panel 2.** Lina tests the sample.

LINA: Then we make one unit that treats this water safely.

**Panel 3.** Jalen, Suri and Ren connect their components. The systems fail to communicate.

**Panel 4.** The readout drops to `11% SIMULATED EFFICIENCY.`

SFX: CHUFF

REN: Smaller failure.

PAGE CHECK: Their goal is a usable customer-serving product, not a grand solution.

---
```

## Page 43 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **LOCK** — Director Cassian Lock is a silver-templed Northern European-descended man in his early sixties, tall and lean with light skin, pale blue-grey eyes, a smooth square jaw and immaculate charcoal hair combed back from a high forehead. He wears a charcoal suit with a platinum collar pin, polished boots and no House crest; his silhouette is long, clean and deliberately neutral, accompanied when necessary by discreet aides rather than visible force. His signature detail is his habit o
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen, lean and mechanically capable, with a mixed Indigenous Andean and European-descended face that clearly resembles Adrian around the jaw and eyes without making him a younger copy; his medium-brown skin, dark wavy hair cut short at the sides, deep brown eyes, straight nose, and narrow, work-hardened face carry both family histories without making ancestry a visual shorthand for character. He is average height with a wiry build, slightly hunched shoulder
- **SURI** — Suri Anik is a South Asian or mixed South Asian/Latin American woman in her early thirties, slim and tall with medium-brown skin, black hair braided close to the scalp, dark focused eyes and a calm oval face. She wears a faded green biofilter research coat over a rust-coloured shirt, rolled trousers, rubberized work shoes and clear protective sleeves; her silhouette is narrow and vertical among root beds, algae columns and abandoned desks. Her signature detail is a small living
- **JALEN** — Jalen Orr is an Afro-descendant Afro-Latin man in his late twenties, tall and wiry with deep brown skin, close-cropped black hair, dark alert eyes and a narrow face that rarely stops scanning the room. He wears a dark Vector House compute-core coverall with the sleeves rolled high, a heat-resistant vest, cable clips at his belt and insulated boots; his silhouette is angular and quick among server racks and conduits. His signature detail is the small rear-facing mirror clipped t
- **REN** — Ren Okafor is a woman in her late thirties with a broad, muscular build, deep brown skin, a shaved head, dark eyes and a blunt, self-possessed face; her ancestry is not inferred from her surname. She wears a patched Orbital House fabrication suit with scorch marks, a heat-shielded apron, heavy work boots and a clipped respirator; her silhouette is broad-shouldered, grounded and visibly built for outer-rim machinery.
```text
## Page 43 (right) — The collaborators accept responsibility for a repairable product.

**Panel 1.** Alex cuts a physical sleeve to bridge incompatible standards.

**Panel 2.** Suri installs a bounded bypass around the living filter.

SURI: If this part fails, the whole unit stops. That’s safer.

**Panel 3.** Lina marks a testing schedule. Ren adds service access panels. Jalen records the failure limits.

**Panel 4.** A calendar page turns through several weeks: fabrication, testing and installation. The compact purifier runs steadily.

DISPLAY: `91% SIMULATED EFFICIENCY.`  
`FIELD TEST — NOT POTABLE-OUTPUT GUARANTEE.`

PAGE CHECK: Time passes between proof and sale. The unit is not presented as finished or magical.

---
```

## Page 45 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. The future is mostly present-day infrastructure aged, repaired and institutionalized. Show systems through pressure, wear, queues, tools, records, gates, surveillance and consequences rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed Andean high-desert basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, mestizo, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live in all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual differences.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded but dignified housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; bronze, gold and engineered green; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, House checkpoints and industrial terraces.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; pearl-white architecture, reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit and civic interiors concealing service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or human-like consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber control nodes, cold administrative displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and procedural language.

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **SURI** — Suri Anik is a South Asian or mixed South Asian/Latin American woman in her early thirties, slim and tall with medium-brown skin, black hair braided close to the scalp, dark focused eyes and a calm oval face. - **JALEN** — Jalen Orr is an Afro-descendant Afro-Latin man in his late twenties, tall and wiry with deep brown skin, close-cropped black hair, dark alert eyes and a narrow face that rarely stops scanning the room. ## Page 46 (left) — Ada confronts her mother.

```text
**Panel 1.** In a civic archive, Leona Veyra stands among old records. Ada stops several steps away.

LEONA: You’re taller.

ADA: That’s what you have?

**Panel 2.** Leona opens a stone-grey box containing a sage-gold Covenant band.

LEONA: Ada. My daughter.

**Panel 3.** Leona looks at the child’s object inside the box.

LEONA: I knew where you were within a year.

ADA: Then you knew enough to come.

**Panel 4.**

LEONA: I believed you were safer not knowing I was looking.

ADA: Those aren’t the same thing.

PAGE CHECK: The mother-daughter relationship is explicit.
```

Alex remains visibly shaped by Keel even as he moves upward. B11 is rugged, foldable, repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, dry, questioning and fallible—never a lecturer, threat or substitute for human relationships. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

```text
## Page 47 (right) — Leona reveals the vote that gave Alpha emergency authority.

**Panel 1.** An archival projection shows younger Leona, Adrian and Mera beside three Covenant bands.

LEONA: We built the Covenant so communities could make agreements without handing everything to one authority.

**Panel 2.** The projected bands show Adrian’s refusal, Mera’s approval and Leona’s undecided band.

**Panel 3.**

LEONA: A shortage broke one agreement. Retaliation followed. Mera voted yes. Adrian voted no.

**Panel 4.** Leona holds her band.

LEONA: I cast the deciding vote for temporary emergency authority.

PAGE CHECK: The history is attached to Leona’s personal responsibility.
```
